Module only: the built-in placeholder weights until the trained file (L152). The gateway loads it from `crews` (L153). |
| Crew routing (task-level multi-model routing, Pareto crewing) | not in Bifrost | done (Raiju-only) | `crewmath.tov`: the formulas (role cost, per-role argmax and coordinate ascent, frontier and knee, AIQ, pacing, credibility, reservation index, verifier threshold, within-task posterior, sequential step). L153, `crew.tov`: the `crews` config (pools, candidates, subscriptions, profiles, priors and classifier as files or inline objects, operating point, credibility) checked at start; `crew/<pool>` models on every route that goes through the engine, with `x-raiju-task-id`, `x-raiju-crew-role` and `x-raiju-task-class`/`-labels`/`-attempt`; a task's crew fixed on its first call and kept on the cluster state (ns `crew.task`, `task_ttl_seconds`); the answers' `x-raiju-crew-candidate`, `-class` and `-attempt`; log rows with `task_id`, `crew_pool`, `crew_role`, `crew_class`, `crew_candidate`, `crew_attempt`; learned profiles and subscription quotas from the node's log rows; `GET /api/crews`, `POST /api/crews/{pool}/assign`, `GET /api/crews/tasks/{task_id}?pool=`. L154: `POST /api/crews/tasks/{task_id}/outcome?pool=` (quality, verifier pass or fail, attempt, accuracy; the first outcome of an attempt counts; the attempt's cost summed from its log rows; attempts kept on the assignment); online credibility (eq. 10) per class, role and candidate from the install's own evidence, each writer's counts under its own key on ns `crew.cells` and in its file in the logs folder, the model summing the writers; crew-level means in the listing; budget pacing (eq. 12) per writer on ns `crew.lambda`, the pool's multiplier the mean of the live writers, with the running mean cost; `POST /api/crews/tasks/{task_id}/next?pool=` (Pandora's rule on the within-task posterior over the frontier's crews, Theorem 2's cutoff, accept or the next attempt's crew, calls carrying the attempt routed to it); expired assignments tombstoned when read or on a sweep; a candidate's `reasoning_effort` set on its routed chat body. L155: the Crews tab (`web/src/screens/Crews.tsx`, `FrontierChart` in `charts.tsx`): per pool the candidates, subscriptions, pacing and classes; per class the frontier with its knee and multiplier, the chosen crew, profiles and learned evidence; the learned role effects sorted; a task lookup; assign, outcome and next for writers only. RBAC resource `Crews` (View reads, Create writes; `rbac.tov`). On `GET /metrics`, the `raiju_crew_*` families (`metrics.tov`): assignments, calls, attempts, escalations, accepts, outcomes with quality, task cost, multiplier and quota gauges (the two gauges merge by maximum across workers). The diagonal (uniform crews) is not drawn: the listing does not give it. Limits in `docs/crew-routing.md`. Design: `docs/crew-routing.md`. |

## Gaps

Ordered by importance. Each gap says what to build and where Bifrost defines it. "Ledger task" means it can become a row in `LEDGER.md`.

### P0

- **G1. Async poll path and status names.** Build `GET /v1/async/<op>/{job_id}` for every submitted op, returning the same job JSON. Keep `GET /v1/async/jobs/{id}` as an alias. Use Bifrost's status names: pending, processing, completed, failed (`core/schemas/async.go:18-21`). Add the missing ops: audio/transcriptions, images/edits, images/variations, ocr, rerank. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/handlers/asyncinference.go`, `docs/features/async-inference.mdx` "Supported Endpoints". Raiju: `src/server.tov:203`, `src/jobs.tov:22`, `162-170`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 49ac636). All listed ops and Bifrost poll paths are in place.

- **G2. Inference auth default and config keys.** Read `client.enforce_auth_on_inference` and top-level `auth_config.is_enabled`, and default to true for new configs (Bifrost's schema says true on new deployments and when the first admin is created; an explicit false opts out). Today Raiju's gate defaults to false (`src/governance.tov:85`), so a Bifrost config without the key lets anonymous inference through. Decide whether existing configs with the key omitted keep open behaviour. Bifrost: `transports/config.schema.json` (`client.enforce_auth_on_inference`, `auth_config`). Ledger task, with a decision. **Status: Done.** `auth_config.is_enabled` and `disable_auth_on_inference` are honoured. The gate now defaults to on when credentials exist (`src/governance.tov:121`). Commit 32da2e6.

- **G3. guardrails_config is ignored.** Bifrost's `guardrails_config` (`guardrail_rules`, `guardrail_providers`) is not read; Raiju reads `guardrails`. A Bifrost config therefore runs with no guardrails and no warning. Implement the Bifrost shape, or at least fail loudly. Bifrost: `transports/config.schema.json` `$defs/guardrails_config`, `docs/enterprise/guardrails.mdx`. Raiju: `src/config.tov` `GuardrailsConfig`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 0c999ff). Read through `src/guardrails_compat.tov`.

- **G4. Budgets and rate limits do not enforce for models outside the built-in price table.** `pricing.tov` covers 15 models. `cost()` returns 0 for any other model (`src/pricing.tov:56-58`), so a budget on those models never runs down. Add a Bifrost-compatible price catalog (default datasheet `framework/modelcatalog/config.go:109`, `pricing_url` and `pricing_sync_interval` in `config.schema.json` framework.pricing), plus `governance.pricing_overrides` (`docs/providers/custom-pricing.mdx`). Until then, refuse or warn when a budgeted request hits an unpriced model. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 0c999ff; switch in L95). Built-in datasheet and opt-in sync. Models outside the datasheet still cost 0 and skip budgets, with a one-time warning, as Bifrost does. `client.refuse_unpriced_models` (off by default) refuses budgeted requests to them with 403.

- **G5. OpenAI SDK route is incomplete.** Mount `/openai/v1/audio/*`, `/openai/v1/images/*`, `/openai/v1/files*`, `/openai/v1/batches*`, `/openai/v1/responses/{id}` (GET, DELETE, cancel, input_items), `/openai/v1/responses/input_tokens`, `/openai/v1/responses/compact`, `/openai/v1/models/{id}`, and the deployment variants for embeddings, images, audio, responses and models. `compatPath` should map `/openai/v1/` onto the `/v1/` routes, as it already does for `/litellm/` and `/langchain/`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/openai.go` (`CreateOpenAIRouteConfigs`, paths `/openai/v1/...`), `docs/openapi/openapi.json`. Raiju: `src/server.tov:167-202`, `171-194`, `src/media.tov:23-41`. Ledger task. **Status: Done.** `/openai/deployments/{d}/*` for chat (streaming too), completions, embeddings, responses (input_tokens, compact), images (generations, edits, variations) and audio; the models variant is not registered by Bifrost either.

- **G6. Bedrock SDK streaming routes.** Add `POST /bedrock/model/{id}/converse-stream` and `/invoke-with-response-stream`, with event-stream framing. Reuse `bedrock.tov` `streamRequest` (`src/providers/index.tov:70`). Today `bedrockModel` returns 404 for any action other than converse or invoke (`src/native.tov:234`). Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/bedrock.go:68-125` and `118-200`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 0b03e53).

- **G7. Config sections that load silently under a different name, or not at all.** Bifrost names and Raiju names differ for these, and Raiju ignores Bifrost's name without a warning: **Status: Partial.** Mapped: alerting, audit_logs, cluster_config, circuit_breaker_config, load_balancer_config, framework.pricing, mcp.virtual_mcps, and the otel and semanticcache plugin entries (commits 0c999ff, 0d269dd, d91ef07, abf4dbb). The logging, telemetry and governance plugins map onto the client section; routing, compat and prompts load with a note for each setting Raiju doesn't implement (`src/config_compat.tov` mapPlugins). **Status now: Done** for config loading; the compat and prompts features themselves are G37 and G46.
  - `alerting` (Raiju: `alerts`), `audit_logs` (Raiju: `audit`), `cluster_config` (Raiju: `cluster`), `circuit_breaker_config` and `load_balancer_config` (Raiju: `health`), `framework.pricing` (Raiju: `pricing`), `mcp.virtual_mcps` (Raiju: top-level `virtual_mcps`), and `plugins[]` entries named `semanticcache`, `otel`, `logging`, `telemetry`, `governance`, `compat`, `routing`, `prompts` (only `header`, `mocker`, `jsonparser`, `blocklist` and `maxtokens` load).
  - Build a loader that maps the Bifrost names, and that reports every unrecognised key at startup. Bifrost: `transports/config.schema.json`. Raiju: `src/config.tov` loaders, `src/plugins.tov:347-372`. Ledger task.

### P1

- **G8. Cohere SDK routes.** Add `/cohere/v2/chat`, `/cohere/v2/embed`, `/cohere/v2/rerank`, `/cohere/v1/tokenize`, and the litellm and langchain variants. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/cohere.go:73-200`. Raiju: `src/providers/cohere.tov` has the conversions; the routes are missing from `src/server.tov`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 0b03e53).

- **G9. `/pydanticai/*` prefix.** Add it to `compatPath` (`src/server.tov:171-175`). Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/pydanticai.go:25-37`. One-line change. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 896b797).

- **G10. Anthropic SDK: models, batches, files.** Add `GET /anthropic/v1/models`, `/anthropic/v1/messages/batches` (create, list, get, cancel, results), `/anthropic/v1/files` (upload, list, get, content, delete). Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/anthropic.go` lines 321-330, 1078-1300, 1468-1700. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commits 6e05a2d, c363369). Models, batches (including results) and files are routed in `src/anthropic_batch.tov`.

- **G11. GenAI SDK: cross-provider and missing routes.** Convert GenAI requests to the target provider, not only Gemini (`src/native.tov:223`). Add `GET /genai/v1beta/models` and `/models/{m}`, the operations route, files (with the upload route), batches, cachedContents, and `/genai/v1/rank`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/genai.go` (`CreateGenAIRouteConfigs`, file, batch and cached routes, lines 45-1395). Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commits 0b03e53, 6f3cbcd). generateContent, streaming, embeddings, countTokens, models, files (with resumable upload), batches, cachedContents, operations, :predict and /genai/v1/rank. Cached contents and operations are Gemini-only; non-Gemini batches convert generateContent requests only.

- **G12. Bedrock SDK: cross-provider, count-tokens and rerank.** Accept non-Bedrock targets by converting Converse to chat and back (`src/native.tov:240` rejects them today). Add `/bedrock/model/{id}/count-tokens` and `/bedrock/rerank`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/bedrock.go:287-360` and the request converters. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commits 0b03e53 and the Bedrock rerank commit). Converse and invoke to any provider with streaming; count-tokens calls Bedrock's API for Bedrock targets (estimate elsewhere, marked); `/bedrock/rerank` and Bedrock as a /v1/rerank provider.

- **G13. Responses lifecycle and related routes.** Implement retrieve, delete, cancel and input_items for `/v1/responses/{id}` (OpenAI first), plus `/v1/responses/input_tokens` and `/v1/responses/compact`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/handlers/inference.go` (responses routes) and `integrations/openai.go:763-953`. Raiju: `src/server.tov:215`, `src/native.tov:187`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 12757de).

- **G14. Model retrieve.** Add `GET /v1/models/{model}` and `/openai/v1/models/{model}`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/handlers/inference.go` (modelRetrieve), `integrations/openai.go:1770-1772`. Raiju: `src/server.tov:202`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (L105 for Cohere). Commit 896b797 added the route. L97e answers with provider metadata for OpenAI-format, Anthropic and Gemini; L105 adds Cohere's model object. Azure is not built: Bifrost's Azure ModelRetrieve is unsupported, so Azure keeps the list entry, as Bedrock does (see the a1 row for `GET /v1/models/{model}`). That is a decision for the owner if a retrieve is wanted.

- **G15. Realtime WebRTC, client secrets and sessions.** Add `POST /v1/realtime/calls`, `/v1/realtime/client_secrets`, `/v1/realtime/sessions`, and the `/openai/` variants. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/handlers/webrtcrealtime.go`, `realtime_client_secrets.go`, openapi. Raiju: `src/realtime.tov` (WebSocket only). Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 12757de).

- **G16. Files, batches and media for non-OpenAI providers.** `media.tov:249` rejects providers that are not OpenAI-format or Azure. Add native implementations or pass-through for Anthropic (files, batches), Gemini (speech, STT, images, files, batches), Vertex (images, batches, files), Bedrock (images, files, batches), and Cohere (files). Also add `GET /v1/batches/{id}/results`. Bifrost: `core/providers/<name>/` (BatchCreate, FileUpload, etc.) and `docs/providers/supported-providers/*.mdx`. Ledger task (one per provider). **Status: Done** (L97a, L97e, L97h). Vertex and Bedrock batches and files (GCS and S3) are in place. Cohere has no batch or file API upstream in Bifrost either.

- **G17. Bifrost response header contract.** Send `x-bifrost-provider`, `x-bifrost-original-model`, `x-bifrost-resolved-model`, `x-bifrost-request-type`, `x-bifrost-fallback-index`, `x-bifrost-latency-ms`, `x-bifrost-upstream-latency-ms`, and the cache headers (`x-bifrost-cache-hit`, `-hit-type`, `-similarity`, `-threshold`, and the rest). Add `x-bifrost-error-type` and `x-bifrost-retry-after-ms`. Add `Access-Control-Expose-Headers` for these. Decide whether to emit `extra_fields` in the body or the headers (`client.extra_fields_placement`). Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/lib/responseheaders.go:23-132` and `ExposedResponseHeaders`. Raiju: `src/gateway.tov:886-1008`, `src/server.tov:148-157`. Ledger task. **Status: Partial.** Commit abf4a31 and L97f send the full set, including retry-after-ms, the cache origin headers, routing info, the passthrough path and extra_fields placement. Remaining: `server-side-fallback-model` on streamed Anthropic answers (set on non-streamed answers only). L105 did not build it: Bifrost sends it in each stream chunk's `extra_fields.routing_info` (its stream headers go out before the fallback is known), and Raiju's stream chunks carry no extra_fields, so this needs the chunk shape and the extra_fields placement decided first. `x-bifrost-pricing-request-type` is n/a (see the b2 row).

- **G18. Metric names and label dimensions.** Add `bifrost_success_requests_total`, `bifrost_error_requests_total` (status_code, error_type), `bifrost_stream_first_token_latency_seconds`, `bifrost_stream_inter_token_latency_seconds`, `bifrost_cache_read_input_tokens_total` and `_write_`, and the `http_*` family. Add the enrichment label set (`virtual_key_name`, `team_name`, `customer_name`, `selected_key_name`, `routing_rule_name`, `complexity_tier`, `alias`, `app`, `fallback_index` and the id variants). Keep `raiju_*` as extras. Bifrost: `plugins/telemetry/main.go:420-694`, `core/schemas/enrichment.go:53-92`. Raiju: `src/gateway.tov` call sites, `src/metrics.tov:167-171`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (L97i, L97m). Families, labels, the routing-classifier families, the push gateway, and the overhead metrics with their breakdown. The breakdown's components are Raiju's own four measured stages, not Bifrost's per-span split (see the h rows).

- **G19. Reasoning parameters.** Map `reasoning_effort` and `max_completion_tokens` to Anthropic thinking budgets, Gemini thinking config, and Bedrock Nova settings. Today these fields are dropped for non-OpenAI providers (no `reasoning_effort` handling in `src/`). Bifrost: `docs/providers/reasoning.mdx` ("Provider-Specific Conversions", "Effort vs. Max Tokens"). Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit ac4bf4f).

- **G20. Per-request content logging control.** Read `x-bf-disable-content-logging` and `client.disable_content_logging` (both are missing), and apply them in `src/logstore.tov`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/lib/ctx.go:743`, `config.schema.json` client. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 12757de).

- **G21. Governance objects missing from the management API and config.** `model_configs` (per-model budgets and limits), provider-level governance (`governance.providers`), and `pricing_overrides`. Add the Bifrost routes: `/api/governance/model-configs`, `/api/governance/providers`, `/api/governance/pricing-overrides`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/handlers/governance.go`, `config.schema.json` governance. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 31f4aaa).

- **G22. MCP client and virtual MCP management.** Add `/api/mcp/client` CRUD, reconnect, refresh-tools and the verification routes, and `/api/mcp/virtual-mcps` CRUD with virtual-key attach and detach. Accept `mcp.client_configs` fields that Raiju lacks: `endpoint_slug`, `allow_by_default`, `disabled`, `tools_to_auto_execute` (per client), `tool_sync_interval`, `tls_config`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/handlers/mcp.go`, `handlers/virtualmcp.go`, `config.schema.json` `mcp_client_config`. Raiju: `src/mcp.tov:12-23`, `src/ops.tov:645-690`. Ledger task. **Status: Partial.** Client and virtual MCP CRUD, reconnect, refresh-tools, openapi-spec, verify, reauthorize, reregister and the OAuth and per-user flows are in place (commit 5c2c8af, L91, L97d, L97k, L97o). Remaining: the `inprocess` connection type (the `token_exchange` runtime connection is done in L133). Bifrost's inprocess client needs a Go `*server.MCPServer` instance, which JSON config cannot carry (`InProcessServer` is `json:"-"`), and Raiju has no Go SDK, so the type stays unsupported: a client set to it fails at connect as an unknown connection type (LIMITS in L110).

Routing CEL variables and regex.** Add the Bifrost variables (`virtual_key_name`, `virtual_key_id`, `team_name`, `team_id`, `customer_name`, `customer_id`, `request_type` values) and `params`. Bifrost's `matches` takes regular expressions. Rules written for Bifrost that use `*_name` currently fail to compile (`src/cel.tov:397`, IDENTS at `cel.tov:78`). Bifrost: `docs/providers/routing-rules.mdx` "Available Variables". Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit abf4dbb).

Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 6aeb7f2).

Add the `bedrock_mantle` provider (G27). Bifrost: `transports/config.schema.json` `bedrock_key`, `docs/providers/supported-providers/bedrock-mantle.mdx`. Raiju: `src/config.tov` BedrockKeyConfig. Ledger task. **Status: Done.** role_arn, external_id, session_name, inference-profile arn, use_openai_endpoints, and the batch role and S3 settings (commit e572dc3).

These matter for private endpoints (vLLM, Ollama and in-house gateways). Bifrost: `transports/config.schema.json` `network_config`, `proxy_config`. Raiju: `src/config.tov` NetworkConfig, `providers/types.tov`. Confirm first whether Tov's fetch can take a CA bundle. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit b66cf96).

Bifrost: `core/providers/bedrockmantle/`, `docs/providers/supported-providers/bedrock-mantle.mdx`. For the rest, return an error rather than a BM25 ranking, so clients do not get different results silently. Bifrost: `core/providers/vllm/`, `bedrock/`, `cloudflare/` (Rerank), `docs/providers/supported-providers/vllm.mdx`. Raiju: `src/extras.tov:27-55`. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit 12757de).

Raiju: `src/otel.tov` (traces only). Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commits 0d269dd, abf4dbb, and L92). Plugin options, metrics push, per-signal headers, and OTLP gRPC (plaintext h2c; TLS through the ALPN patch).

Semantic cache config and backends.** Accept Bifrost's `semanticcache` plugin entry (`plugins[]`) with `dimension`, `vector_store_namespace`, `cache_tool_call_responses`, `disable_identity_scoping`. Add at least one external vector store (Redis, Qdrant, Weaviate or Pinecone). Bifrost: `plugins/semanticcache/main.go:32-61`, `docs/integrations/vector-databases/*.mdx`. Raiju: `src/semcache.tov` (in-process only). Raiju: `src/alerts.tov:6, 22` (`error_rate`, `p95_latency_ms`, `avg_latency_ms`, `requests`, `cost`, `budget_used`, `breaker_open`; channels `webhook`, `slack`). Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commits abf4dbb and 8e98ea5).

- **G32. SCOPE.md overstates parity.** Change these rows to partial with the caveats above: OpenAI SDK route, Bedrock SDK route, Google GenAI SDK route, Anthropic SDK route, Audio, Images, Files and batches, Realtime (WebSocket only), config.json compatibility, Prometheus metrics, Azure OpenAI (Entra), and Multi-core workers (metrics and logs per worker). The Out table lists the features that are not built.

Make this visible in the API docs and the SCOPE entry, or add provider-side counting where Bifrost has it (Bedrock count-tokens, Fireworks, SGL, Vertex). Provider-side counting exists for Anthropic, Gemini, Vertex and some Bedrock models; the rest is estimated and marked `"exact": false`.

### P2

- **G34. Direct-key bypass decision.** Bifrost's docs say direct-key bypass was removed in v1.5, but `main` still has `x-bf-direct-key` and `allow_direct_keys` (`transports/bifrost-http/lib/ctx.go:59-60, 894`). Confirm the target Bifrost version. If it is needed, implement `x-bf-direct-key` gated by `client.allow_direct_keys`. Otherwise warn at startup when the key is set. Raiju parses the key and does nothing (`src/config.tov`). **Status: Done** (commit cbd6ad3). Implemented, gated by `client.allow_direct_keys`.

- **G35. Passthrough routes.** `/openai_passthrough`, `/anthropic_passthrough`, `/azure_passthrough`, `/bedrock_passthrough`, `/genai_passthrough`, `/chatgpt_passthrough`, `/runware_passthrough`. Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/passthrough.go:184-261`. Not in `SCOPE.md`. **Status: Done** (commit 6b82d47). Six prefixes are done. `/runware_passthrough` is a media provider and is out of scope (SCOPE.md Out).

- **G36. Decisions API and typesafe provider.** `/v1/decisions`, `/openai/v1/decisions`, `/typesafe/v1/systemone`, `/typesafe/v1/models`, and a decision-model complexity classifier (Laya, Nimble). Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/integrations/typesafe.go`, `docs/providers/self-hosted-decision-models.mdx`, `plugins/routing`. Not in `SCOPE.md`. **Status: Partial** (L81, L109). Built: `/v1/decisions`, the typesafe provider, the decision-model classifier, and decision emulation under `client.compat.decisions`. L109 added the unprefixed `/decisions` routes (`/openai/decisions`, and under `/litellm`, `/langchain` and `/pydanticai`) and decision pricing overrides: an override's `request_types` choose the decision or the other billing, and its `input_cost_per_token_decisions` and `output_cost_per_token_decisions` bill decisions. L163 added the native decision routes of OpenAI (gpt-6-luna), Azure, Cloudflare, Databricks, Perplexity and Ollama (`providers/decision_native.tov`). Open: the datasheet's own decision rates, which Raiju's datasheet does not carry.

- **G37. Compat plugin.** Implement `x-bf-compat`, `client.compat`, parameter dropping and conversion, text-to-chat, chat-to-responses, reasoning-only models, and decision emulation. Bifrost: `plugins/compat/` (convertparams.go, dropparams.go, azure.go), `docs/features/compat-plugin.mdx`. Response headers `x-bifrost-dropped-compat-params` and `x-bifrost-dropped-unsupported-tools` depend on it. **Status: Done** (L82, L100, L107, L109, L163). Built: the compat plugin, `x-bf-compat`, parameter dropping and conversion, text-to-chat and chat-to-responses streams, reasoning-only models, and decision emulation. Streamed answers send the compat headers (L107 checked the chat, text, Responses, legacy and cursor streams). L109 added the native-decision model check for OpenRouter under compat. L163 added the native-decision checks for OpenAI, Azure, Cloudflare, Databricks, Perplexity and Ollama.

- **G38. Missing providers.** Add `databricks`, `cloudflare`, `github-copilot`, `sarvam`, `wafer`, `opencode-go`, `opencode-zen`, `replicate` (chat only), and `typesafe`. Bifrost: `core/schemas/bifrost.go:49-82`, `docs/providers/supported-providers/`. `elevenlabs`, `runway` and `runware` are media providers and out of scope. **Status: Done** (commit d27a62e; typesafe in L81). The listed chat providers are in, with Replicate as chat only, as Bifrost lists it.

- **G39. Raw request and response handling.** Implement `x-bf-send-back-raw-request`, `x-bf-send-back-raw-response`, `x-bf-store-raw-request-response`, and the provider-level `send_back_raw_*` keys (parsed but unused in Raiju). Bifrost: `transports/bifrost-http/lib/ctx.go:715-737`, `config.schema.json` provider. Ledger task. **Status: Done** (commit cbd6ad3; store L110). Send-back for request and response, per provider and per request, is done. `x-bf-store-raw-request-response` keeps the upstream request and response in the log (per request under `client.allow_per_request_content_storage_override`, else the provider's `store_raw_request_response`). L110 added the streamed answers that go straight to the client: their upstream body is kept up to 1 MiB, unless output guards apply. Both are dropped when content logging is off for the request (`x-bf-disable-content-logging`, the key or the client setting), as Bifrost's logging plugin does.

- **G40. Live, WebSocket responses, and the UI socket.** `/v1/responses` WebSocket mode (`handlers/wsresponses.go`), `/v1/live/sessions` (`handlers/wslive.go`, `livecontrol.go`), and `/ws` (`handlers/websocket.go`). Not in `SCOPE.md`. **Status: Partial** (L83, L102, L108). Built: the Responses WebSocket with events streamed as they arrive and response.cancel, `/v1/live/sessions` for OpenAI with attach and content, the `/ws` UI socket with heartbeat, and registries shared across workers. L108 added the `/v1/realtime` relay for xAI (Bifrost's RealtimeProvider set is OpenAI, Azure, xAI and ElevenLabs; ElevenLabs is out of scope) and a WebRTC create on POST /v1/live/sessions that passed the browser's offer to OpenAI. L122 replaced that with Bifrost's design: Raiju is the WebRTC peer (libdatachannel in the Tov runtime, `tov-patches/0006`, `std/rtc`), answers the browser's offer on its own peer connection, offers to OpenAI on a second, and relays the audio RTP and the `oai-events` data channel, with every event metered, guarded and logged (`live.tov` `liveCreate`, `webrtc.tov`). Bifrost's GPT Live is OpenAI only, so no other provider gets it; Gemini has no realtime API in Bifrost. L128 put every realtime and live session through that one core. The WebSocket path of live sessions (`LiveSocket`), the `/v1/realtime` WebSocket for OpenAI, Azure and xAI (`RealtimeSocket`, on both servers) and the SDP calls of `/v1/realtime` and `/v1/realtime/calls` (now answered by Raiju's peer too, `realtimecalls.tov`) are metered, admitted per window or turn, guarded and logged like the WebRTC create, and the sessions are billed: a live session its voice seconds at `input_cost_per_second` plus its delegated tokens, a realtime session the `response.done` usage with the audio token rates, the cache rate and the text rates (`pricing.tov` `Price.inputAudio`, `outputAudio`, `perSecond`, `usageParts`, `voiceCost`; the datasheet parser, `config.pricing`, `governance.pricing_overrides` and `scripts/gen_pricing.py` carry the new fields). L137 gave every session Bifrost's per-unit accounting: a live session is a row per window of voice time, per delegated response and one that ends it (`livehooks.tov` `unitMeta`, `webrtc.tov` `LiveCore.emit` and `TurnReport`), a realtime session a row per turn, every row with its own tokens, audio seconds and cost, its own request id and the session as its parent request id; metrics and the live feed see each unit as it closes, and `Engine.recordLive` charges its cost and tokens to the budgets and token limits then, so a long session cannot spend past a budget before it ends (the next window or turn is admitted first and refused as admission does). L160 added client secrets as Bifrost mints them (the provider's token replaced by the gateway's `ek_bf_` token, mapped on the cluster state to the virtual key and pool key until it expires; a WebSocket or call presenting it is that virtual key's session, the provider sent its own token), transcription sessions (`?intent=transcription` bootstrapped from the first update, calls of type transcription; a row per transcription at the transcription rates) and the status of a failed (Bifrost's realtime error status) or cancelled (499) `response.done`. Open: the committed price table takes its audio and per-second rates from LiteLLM's public price file (L135, `scripts/gen_pricing.py --merge`), but LiteLLM's OpenAI live models (`gpt-live-1`, `gpt-live-transcribe`) are not rows of it, so a live voice second costs 0 until a refreshed datasheet, an override or `config.pricing` names one; only a live session bills voice seconds (Bifrost's `LiveRequest`), a realtime session is billed by its tokens (`RealtimeRequest`); a delegated response that finishes after the session was refused is billed on the unit that ends it, and the empty unit Bifrost closes with each lane at the end is not logged; no STUN/TURN servers unless `RAIJU_WEBRTC_ICE_SERVERS` names them (Bifrost uses none either).

- **G41. Projects, business units and custom RBAC.** `governance.projects`, `governance.business_units`, `governance.roles`, `x-bf-project-id`, `x-bf-customer-id`, custom roles and permissions, and data access scopes. Bifrost: `handlers/governance.go`, `docs/enterprise/projects.mdx`, `data-access-control.mdx`. Enterprise scope decision. **Status: Partial** (L84, L97p, L103). Built: projects with members, split policies and entity_dac; business units; custom roles; data access scopes; x-bf-project-id and x-bf-customer-id; rate-limit shares per member (L103); the role-id API shape (L103); access profiles attached to roles, with the held profiles governing their holders' keys (L103); budget override cycles kept across restarts (L103). Open: built-in roles' ids, the paying-profile rule for held profiles, and the redivision job (see the access profile and project rows).

- **G42. Log sinks and export formats.** BigQuery, Kafka, Pub/Sub, Maxim sinks; Parquet payloads; export retention policy. Bifrost: `docs/features/observability/*.mdx`, `docs/enterprise/log-exports.mdx`. Raiju: `src/exports.tov` (JSONL only). **Status: Done** (L85, L89). BigQuery, Kafka, Pub/Sub and Maxim sinks, Parquet payloads and export retention are in place.

- **G43. Guardrail breadth.** Secrets detection (Gitleaks rule set), regex guardrails, reversible redaction, prompt guardrails (LLM judge), MCP tool-call guard, and the Presidio, Azure Language PII, Google Model Armor and CrowdStrike AIDR providers. Bifrost: `docs/enterprise/guardrails*.mdx`, `docs/integrations/guardrails/*`. Raiju: `src/guardrails.tov`, `src/guardrail_providers.tov`. **Status: Partial** (L86, L90, L93, L101, L107, L111, L118, L131). Built: regex guardrails, the Gitleaks rule set with the secrets engine, reversible redaction, the LLM judge, the MCP tool-call guard, Presidio, Azure PII, Model Armor and CrowdStrike. Output guards on text completion and Responses streams, with redaction on streams, are built (L97s). L107 adds the built-in guard on streams, redaction of a match across a release point, and replay pacing of cached streams. `gitleaks:allow` comments are honoured (L111). L131 adds Bifrost's hold-and-replay of live block-capable streams (held until the guards pass, replayed paced by stream_replay_event_interval_ms, bounded by Raiju's hold caps). The secrets scan is fast on text that holds every rule keyword (L118): the regex engine runs integer opcodes with a first-character skip, a rule with a required literal (regex.tov literals()) tests only the windows and starts that literal can reach, and each window is decoded once. Bench (guard_secrets.tov, 20 KB): keyword-dense code 22.5 ms best / 24.9 ms mean before, 5.5 / 5.9 after; every-keyword text 841 ms / 919 ms before, 16.1 / 18.9 after; findings identical to HEAD on 2400 fixtures placed around window seams and on random texts.

- **G44. Clustering discovery and leader election.** Kubernetes, DNS, Consul, etcd, mDNS and UDP discovery; leader election; broker mode. Bifrost: `transports/config.schema.json` `cluster_config`, `docs/enterprise/clustering.mdx`. Raiju: `src/cluster.tov`, static peers only. **Status: Done** (L87, L92, L94). Kubernetes, DNS, Consul, etcd, mDNS and UDP discovery, the etcd lease leader lock, and broker mode (an HTTP relay, not Bifrost's gRPC).

## Remaining gaps (current as of the L97 waves)

Each item is still open after L97 and L101 to L102; the gap above gives the detail. No P2 product decision is open. What is left is partial builds.

1. G43. Hold-and-replay is built (L131); the hold is bounded by Raiju's own caps, which Bifrost's docs do not describe.
2. G40. The committed price table has audio rates from LiteLLM's price file (L135) and no OpenAI live model with a voice rate per second, since gpt-live-1 is not in it (L128, L135).
3. G36. The datasheet's own decision rates (native decision routes done in L163).
4. G41. The redivision job, and Bifrost's rule of one paying profile for held profiles (kept by design, owner decision 4 after wave 14). Built-in roles have ids (L165) and are editable (L175).
5. G48. Vertex AWS workload identity needs a cloud account. (Large requests to providers whose format differs are converted while streaming since L136, and Messages and embeddings since L146, except the cases listed in the large_payload_optimization row.)
6. G22. The `inprocess` connection type is not expressible in Raiju (Bifrost needs a Go server object, which JSON config cannot carry).
7. G14. Azure model retrieve: not built, because Bifrost refuses it (its ModelRetrieve is unsupported). Cohere is done (L105).
8. G17. `server-side-fallback-model` on streamed Anthropic answers.

Out of scope (SCOPE.md Out): videos and containers, the edge device agent, media providers, Warp, the skills marketplace, Sidekiq, and Runware.
